<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The proxy is very unstable. It connected initially (we see it accessed c4 streaming data and resolved some URLs) but then disconnects. The `Network is unreachable` errors persist. The HuggingFace datasets library is having trouble with the C4 dataset which requires downloading loading scripts.

I should try a different approach:
1. Pre-download the wikitext dataset to the data disk
2. Or use `--dataset alpaca` which might be smaller and easier

Actually, the issue seems to be the AutoDL proxy is very unreliable. Let me try downloading the wikitext dataset first in a foreground process with the proxy, then run calibration offline.


Even though I set the proxy environment variables before running nohup, they apparently weren't being propagated consistently to the child process. The persistent "Network is unreachable" errors suggest the proxy connection was dropping. I'll download the wikitext dataset upfront in an interactive session to cache it locally, then run calibration without relying on the network.
```

</details>

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - CARC (Context-Aware Redundancy Classification) MVP validation
   - EMA anchor-based KV similarity scoring with per-head cosine similarity (8 KV heads for this model)
   - TEAL sparsification (magnitude-based activation pruning at 40%/55% levels)
   - Welford online variance algorithm for variance-aware head weighting
   - 5 measurements: MLP near-zero fractions, perturbation@55%, EMA↔surprisal correlation, PPL two-tier vs uniform, position-residualized correlations
   - DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B: 14.8B params, 48 layers, Qwen2MLP architecture
   - AutoDL server constraints: 30G root partition, 250G data disk at `/root/autodl-tmp/`
   - Network proxy: `source /etc/network_turbo` → http_proxy=http://10.37.1.23:12798, but unreliable
   - HF mirror: `HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com` as fallback
   - conda environment at `/root/miniconda3/`

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `/root/carc/carc_utils.py` (419 lines, on server)
     - Shared utilities used by calibrate and MVP scripts
     - Classes: WelfordVariance, EMAAnchorManager (per-head and concatenated modes)
     - Functions: sparsify, compute_perturbation, threshold_from_histogram, residualize, load_math_data, load_eval_data
     - load_eval_data supports c4, wikitext, alpaca datasets

   - `/root/carc/carc_calibrate.py` (187 lines, on server)
     - TEAL calibration: LayerHistogram and HistogramCollector classes with forward hooks on MLP projections
     - CLI args: `--model, --output, --num-samples, --seq-len, --dataset, --batch-size, --num-bins, --dry-run, --dtype`
     - Collects per-layer MLP activation histograms, computes magnitude thresholds at 40% and 55% sparsity
     - Output: JSON with per-layer, per-component thresholds

   - `/root/carc/carc_mvp.py` (28682 bytes on server, with modifications)
     - MVP validation with 5 measurements across 4 forward passes per chain
     - Server-confirmed modifications: `--data-seed` (line 70), `--save-records` (line 71)
     - Line 263: `load_math_data(num_problems=args.num_samples, seed=args.data_seed)` uses data_seed
     - Line 629: npz save block enabled by `--save-records`
     - Classes: MLPCapture (4 modes: baseline, sparse_55, sparse_40, sparse_twotier), KVCapture
     - judge() function: 2x2 decision matrix (PASS-Strong, PASS-Suggestive, DISCORDANT-A/B, FAIL)

   - `/root/carc/merge_results.py` (6820 bytes on server)
     - Merges two MVP result sets from npz + json files
     - CLI: `--npz1, --npz2, --json1, --json2, --output, --tau`

   - Model files at `/root/autodl-tmp/carc/models/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B/`:
